- **p**: Shortcut for "parallelize" — take the work in flight (or the named work), split off the parts that do not depend on each other, and run them at the same time so it finishes sooner. "p" splits what clearly splits, "pp" splits harder, "ppp" is maximum parallelism; "p1", "p2", "p3" are the same three levels. "ff" and "fff" are levels 2 and 3 too; a single "f" is the fast-mode toggle, not a level. Bare forms mean the same thing. ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/commands/p.md))

- **p5-polish**: Collects small clean-up TODOs accumulated during the sprint (from `/p5-review`, `/p5-acceptance`, `/p5-bugfix`, TODO/FIXME comments) that do not justify a full story but make the next sprint easier. Triages each item into `polish-now`, `backlog`, `handover`, or `drop`. Optionally executes `polish-now` items directly (TDD mini-cycle, one commit per item). Returns a per-SOTA summary at the end. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-audit-all.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)

([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-audit-article.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-audit-sota**: Audit a SOTA's bibliography. Classify each cited ref by validation state. Optional --purge removes citations to retracted refs from the SOTA (with .bak backup). ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-audit-sota.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-cascade**: Acquire PDFs via the 8-source cascade (11 with opt-in extended sources) for a single ref by slug, or a batch filtered by state. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-cascade.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)

- **paper-trail-doctor**: Audit the registry via the 19 mechanical invariants (I1-I19). Reports ERROR/WARN/INFO violations. Optional auto-fix for safe ones (I4 path, I6 sha256, I9 attempts). ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-doctor.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-identify**: Première passe du pipeline cible refondu (read-only). Produit un rapport d'identification : pour chaque mention citée dans le SOTA, indique si la fiche existe déjà au registre, son état actuel, si elle a un PDF, et l'action recommandée (réutiliser / créer / passer en revue). Ne modifie ni le SOTA ni le registre. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-identify.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-ingest**: Ingest the citations of one SOTA into the registry. Extracts bibliographic sections, parses citations via the citation-parser sub-agent, resolves DOI via Crossref/S2, deduplicates against the registry, creates new candidate refs, and substitutes free-text citations with [[wikilinks]]. Required first step to make a legacy SOTA conform to the paper-trail pipeline. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-ingest.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-ingest-all**: Batch INGEST across the entire vault. Scans all SOTAs, identifies those with bibliographic sections containing free-text citations, then runs /paper-trail:ingest on each one sequentially. Reports per-SOTA summary at the end. The single batch entry point to make a legacy vault fully conform to the paper-trail pipeline. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-ingest-all.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)

- **paper-trail-purge**: Nettoie les wikilinks invalides d'un SOTA (vers refs retracted, refs `_0000_*` orphelines, suffixes numériques `_2_3_4`, fichiers techniques `20_ATLAS/`, `30_DEV/`, `.canvas`, slugs non-bibliographiques). ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-purge.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)

- **papers-index**: Build/refresh the KB's papers ledger — the one file an agent reads to understand the whole paper corpus without re-reading it. Surfaces the reading backlog (readable PDFs not yet read) and the re-fetch queue (missing/unreadable PDFs). The ledger is the idempotency authority: fetch skips papers already present+readable, summarization skips papers whose PDF is unchanged. - **parsimony**: `parsimony` queues an advisory review of the project's current Baseline. Select a registered check with `--measure` (it defaults to `P-adhoc-duplicate`), or pass `--measure all` to run every check this build supports in one job, which stores one Report per measure; each Report records the measure's findings and full eligibility counts. Pass a completed Dry Run job id with `--dry-run` to compare th ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-dispose**: This command reads one finding from a stored Parsimony Report and derives its exact subject, evidence digest, evidence contract, and readable captions. The Report must belong to the project's current Baseline; a Report measured just before an Apply can still answer that its decision is already recorded, but it cannot stage a new one. `--record-type` must be a portable ID from `parsimony record-typ ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-dispose.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-dry-run**: `parsimony --dry-run &lt;job-id&gt;` queues a comparison of one measure before and after the Proposal behind a completed Dry Run job, on a private copy of the project. It does not change the FieldWorks project. Use `--wait` to receive the stored before and after Reports; see [Review grammar parsimony](cmd:parsimony). ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-dry-run.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-dry-run-wait**: `parsimony --dry-run &lt;job-id&gt; --wait` queues the comparison of one measure before and after the Proposal behind a completed Dry Run job, and waits for the stored before and after Reports. It does not change the FieldWorks project. Use `--wait-timeout-ms` to set the wait bound; see [Review grammar parsimony](cmd:parsimony). ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-dry-run-wait.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-expectations**: Reads the saved project's `parsimony-expectations/v1` projection. It lists human-confirmed [reviewed negatives](term:reviewed-negative) by case and revision, then reports FieldWorks' default-human Approved and Disapproved readings in separate sections. Native Disapproved Opinions reject one exact reading and do not become whole-word negatives. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-expectations.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-group**: `parsimony --group grammar|text|all` runs a whole group of Parsimony measures in one job, waits, and returns one stored Report per measure. The Grammar group reads the grammar alone and just works: when the project has no current Baseline it captures one first, and it never recaptures an existing one. The Text group reads the saved Parsimony Selection. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-group.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-latest**: `parsimony latest` names the newest stored Parsimony Report for a project and the bundle it was measured from. It reads the project's store only and does not invoke PanGloss. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-latest.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-measure-all-wait**: `parsimony --measure all --wait` runs every Parsimony measure this build supports in one job and waits for the result. The job copies the current Baseline, has PanGloss import it and write grammar facts, builds and verifies the evidence once, and stores one Report per measure on that one bundle. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-measure-all-wait.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-measures**: `parsimony measures` lists the fixed Parsimony checks registered in this build, their axes, evidence tiers, required capabilities, citations, and recipe links. It reads the catalog and does not invoke PanGloss. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-measures.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-negative-confirm**: This HumanOnly command stages a human-confirmed surface or exact reading negative as Notebook operations in a Draft Proposal. Supply the Notebook `recordTypeId`, stable `caseId`, vernacular `writingSystem`, `form`, `context`, and a `target` with discriminator `"type":"surface"` or `"type":"reading"`. A reading target carries an ordered `morphs` array; each `identity` names the exact `form`, `msa`, ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-negative-confirm.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-negative-retract**: This HumanOnly command stages a retraction revision for a saved reviewed negative. The closed intent names the Notebook `recordTypeId`, `judgmentId`, and every current `expectedHeads` entry as a revision ID and content digest; the composer refuses stale heads. An optional `reason`, human actor, and explicit UTC timestamp can record why the judgment was withdrawn. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-negative-retract.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-record-types**: Lists the saved record types in the project's Notebook with each type's portable ID and readable name. Choose a type by its ID; Motif never picks one by English name. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-record-types.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-retract**: Use the `recordId` of a current ruling record. `--expected-heads` must name every current head and its exact digest; include both heads to resolve a conflict. Motif refuses a stale digest, a superseded record, or a record from another project. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-retract.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-revise**: Use the `recordId` of a current ruling record. `--expected-heads` must name every current head and its exact digest; include both heads to resolve a conflict. Motif refuses a stale digest, a superseded record, or a record from another project. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-revise.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-rule-attempts**: `parsimony --rule-attempts --plan &lt;intents.json&gt;` reads the intents file you would give `compose-batch` and groups the variants it adds by shape. A shape is the variant type, the environments and the spelling change from the main form, such as a lost final vowel. Five or more variants of one shape need a recorded rule attempt: the rule you tried, its prediction test, and why it failed. Without one ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-rule-attempts.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-show**: `parsimony show` reads a previously stored Parsimony Report by id. It reads the saved Report and does not invoke PanGloss. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-show.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-status**: `parsimony status` says whether Parsimony has compared a Proposal's candidate grammar with the Baseline. A committed Trial queues that comparison on its own, behind all other work, so the answer is usually "queued", "running" or "ready" without anyone asking for it. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-status.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-view**: `parsimony view` reads one page from a fixed, documented evidence view in a published bundle. The cursor is bound to the bundle, view, and filters; the command accepts no SQL and does not build missing artifacts. Supply the Report id when reading parser cases so Motif can restore the exact Assessment references. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-view.md) · ★1)
- **parsimony-wait**: `parsimony --wait` queues the requested measure over the current Baseline and waits for its stored Report. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/parsimony-wait.md) · ★1)
